class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1
            
            mid = (l + r) // 2
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + left)
            return left_ind if left_ind != -1 else dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

                return l if lo == hi else -1

Reason in a repair-and-recheck way.

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

Output only the fixed Python code.

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

Let's consider if there is a simpler O(n) fix that is less likely to have hidden bug. We could output:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total = sum(nums)
        left = 0
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            if left == total - left - x:
                return i
            left += x
        return -1

This is the standard solution. It is correct, handles empty? If nums empty, total=0, loop none, return -1. It doesn't preserve D&C but fixes all bugs. Let's decide final code. I think output:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        if not nums:
            return -1
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        if not nums:
            return -1
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        if not nums:
            return -1
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l >= r:
                return -1
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l >= r:
                return -1
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
```

Need maybe if final code should be exactly:

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l >= r:
                return -1
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1
            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])
            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

If I include import, it's above. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l >= r:
                return -1
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l >= r:
                return -1
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
